# 🗂️ Japan 2027 — Nagoya pocket routes

[[Japan 2027 — Nagoya Chapter|← Nagoya chapter / Maps / sources]] · [[Japan 2027 — Seasonal Guides|Seasonal guides]] · [[Japan 2027 — Today|Today]]

**Three flexible cards; no calendar assigned.** Checked 7 October 2026. Two nights provide arrival + one full city day + departure. Three nights add one full outing day using Card 3. Short-stop visitors use only the variant in Card 1. Follow current operator links in the chapter; transport, tickets, opening, and stock remain unconfirmed.

## Card 1 — arrival and a local meal

**Route:** selected Shinkansen → JR Nagoya → actual hotel entrance. If using NGO, take a suitable Meitetsu service → Meitetsu Nagoya → hotel; μSKY needs the additional first-class ticket. Meitetsu and JR have separate gates. From HND/NRT, follow the chapter's airport + rail comparison with a realistic flight-arrival buffer.

**Main block:** check in / confirmed luggage drop → one meal → a short walk near the base. If staying in Sakae and arriving early enough, compare a brief Oasis 21 / tower-area exterior walk. Preserve the full shopping block for tomorrow.

**Food:** one noodle or miso-katsu meal. **Shot:** station arrival → street context → meal detail. **Buy:** none required; save the shared shopping allowance for deliberate choices.

**Short-stop variant:** confirm luggage storage and onward train first → one meal + **one** chosen area if timing permits → collect luggage → correct platform. Osu browsing and a Sakae evening are not both compulsory. With a tight connection, keep the meal near the station. No overnight is counted for this variant; recheck the actual ticket arrangement and any storage cost.

## Card 2 — one city anchor, Osu, and Sakae

**Morning:** hotel → **Nagoya Castle / Hommaru Palace OR Tokugawaen OR Atsuta Jingu**. Choose one. The castle's main keep is currently closed; check available areas and palace rules. For Tokugawaen, add the separate art museum only if opening, ticket, and available time suit.

**Midday:** seated lunch / café break → Osu loop from Osu Kannon or Kamimaezu → limited clothing browsing and optionally Mandarake. Check actual shop hours before heading over. Specific Tamiya kits remain unverified.

**Later:** choose diskunion's **Sakae** branch OR a current PARCO clothing/accessory shortlist, then dinner and a short Oasis 21 / MIRAI TOWER exterior photo walk if energy allows. Use the updated directory: PARCO South Building is scheduled to close 28 February 2027. The remaining activities can be skipped rather than rushed.

**Food:** simple lunch → wings or another selected main meal; optional Aoyagi sweet/café break replaces another snack. **Shot:** morning scene → permitted clothing/record detail → evening architecture. **Buy:** one measured garment OR a record/collectible, plus a small gift only within the shared cap. Your saved size is a starting lead; her size remains open.

**Rain/heat alternative:** replace the morning with Toyota Commemorative Museum, or rebuild a shorter browsing day around SCMAGLEV and Railway Park via Aonami / Kinjofuto. Check each museum calendar; Toyota currently has a Monday closure with a holiday exception. Keep a seated break and reduce outdoor walking.

## Card 3 — extra full day: choose A OR B OR C

### A. Inuyama

**Route:** hotel → Meitetsu Nagoya → suitable dated train to Inuyama Station → castle-town walk → Inuyama Castle if selected → return to the same Nagoya hotel. The operator gives about 20 minutes' walk from the station to the castle.

**Main block:** town streets and castle → seated meal/break → a manageable riverside/view block if conditions suit. Check castle access, opening, and the return service. **Shot:** streets → castle architecture → river context. **Buy:** optional one compact keepsake that replaces another purchase. No Ghibli Park or Arimatsu extension is counted.

### B. Ghibli Park

**Route:** hotel → subway to Fujigaoka → Linimo to Ai-Chikyuhaku Kinen Koen → correct park/area entrance → return. Use the official route instructions and selected ticket's timings.

**Main block:** the areas included in your advance ticket, with meal/break and walking time. This is Ghibli Park in Aichi, separate from Tokyo's Ghibli Museum. Confirm release rules, ticket areas, calendar, entry time, and permitted photography. **Shot:** only permitted park scenes. **Buy:** one gift within the existing allowance if wanted. If a suitable ticket is unavailable, select another branch; a same-day entrance purchase is not assumed.

### C. Stay local / textile alternative

**Route:** selected museum OR a remaining city morning interest → meal → limited café/shopping. For a textile-focused outing instead, choose Arimatsu / its tie-dyeing museum with a separate dated transfer and opening check. Price and confirm any workshop before choosing it.

**Shot:** machinery/exhibit detail where allowed OR town/textile texture. **Buy:** a compact shibori textile is an optional gift lead; stock and price need checking. These choices replace A/B rather than join them.

**Departure day:** collect luggage → selected Nagoya station/gates → dated onward train, or hotel → confirmed NGO airport service with check-in buffer. The two/three-night mainland comparisons keep two separate Tokyo hotel nights before the international departure. Do not put a full extra-day outing ahead of a tight departure connection.

# 🦌 Japan 2027 — Nara chapter

**Klook tours to compare:** [[Japan 2027 — Klook More Cities and Islands#Nara|Nara tour and experience options]]

**Next regional chapter:** [[Japan 2027 — Okinawa Chapter|Okinawa]] — a separate domestic-flight module, with Naha culture and coastal choices rather than another Kansai day trip.

[[Japan 2027 — Seasonal Guides|← Seasonal guides]] · [[Japan 2027 — Nara Pocket Routes|Three pocket routes]] · [[Japan 2027 — Osaka Chapter|Osaka]] · [[Japan 2027 — Seasonal Planning and Bookings|Budget / bookings]]

[[#Google Maps — sights food shopping and transport|📍 Google Maps]] · [[#Foods and café stops|🍵 Food]] · [[#Getting here — Kyoto Osaka and Tokyo airports|🚆 Transport]]

**Research checked:** 7 October 2026. **Working year:** 2027, unconfirmed. This chapter expands your existing optional Nara excursion. Dates, tickets, restaurants, hotel nights, and travel-day opening times remain provisional. The focus is **Nara City**, rather than every destination in Nara Prefecture.

## My recommendation for you

Give Nara **one full, unhurried day from Kyoto or Osaka**. Start with **Todai-ji and Nara Park**, then choose **Kasuga Taisha** for shrine/lantern-path atmosphere **OR Naramachi** for cafés, sweets, and crafts. Your photography interests make a slower route more useful than trying to visit every hall, museum, garden, and hillside.

**One overnight** is optional if you want two distinct outings and an earlier park start. It replaces a Kyoto/Osaka night; it does not expand the trip beyond 14 nights. Park mornings do not require an overnight, and quieter conditions are not guaranteed.

For your original spring plan, **4 April is already a choice among Uji, Nara, Osaka, or another Kyoto day**. This chapter keeps Nara as a choice rather than selecting it for you. In the seasonal guides, use one Kyoto-area day only if it wins over that day's existing activity.

## Main sights and how to choose

| Place / area | Why it fits you | How I would use it |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Todai-ji / Great Buddha Hall** | A major temple visit with architecture and the Great Buddha | First main anchor, with time to look rather than rush |
| **Nara Park** | Park scenery, deer, walking transitions for a short film | Connect selected sights; deer feeding is optional |
| **Nigatsu-do** | A temple-side extension with elevated views | Optional after Todai-ji if steps and energy suit |
| **Kasuga Taisha** | Shrine setting, lantern-lined approach, woodland atmosphere | Main afternoon choice; distinguish general worship from paid special areas |
| **Naramachi / Sarusawa Pond** | Traditional streets, cafés, sweets, compact gifts | Alternative afternoon or second-day block |
| **Kohfukuji** | Temple grounds and selected art/hall visits | Central-area option; check the restoration note below before expecting a pagoda photograph |
| **Isuien Garden** | Garden composition and a slower visual outing | Alternative to another paid attraction, when open |
| **Mt. Wakakusa** | An uphill walking option and city views | Separate active route that replaces other sightseeing |

Primary visitor sources: [Todai-ji](https://www.todaiji.or.jp/en/) · [Nigatsu-do](https://www.todaiji.or.jp/en/information/nigatsudo/) · [Kasuga Taisha visitor information](https://www.kasugataisha.or.jp/en/about_en/basic/) · [Nara Park area guide](https://www.visitnara.jp/destinations/area/nara-park/) · [Naramachi guide](https://www.visitnara.jp/destinations/area/naramachi/) · [Isuien](https://isuien.or.jp/en/garden).

Nara Park is a broad sightseeing area, not one attraction gate. JR Nara and Kintetsu-Nara are different arrival stations; the park is closer to Kintetsu-Nara. Use buses or shorten the loop when the walking load is too much. [Official park access](https://www.visitnara.jp/destinations/area/nara-park/) · [Nara Kotsu visitor transport](https://www.narakotsu.co.jp/language/en/).

### Two current notices that affect your plans

- **Kohfukuji's five-storied pagoda is undergoing major conservation.** Do not build your shot list around an unobstructed pagoda or its reflection in Sarusawa Pond. Check the [temple-linked conservation project](https://kohfukuji-project.jp/five-storied_pagoda/en/index.html) and [city visitor notice](https://narashikanko.or.jp/en/spot/detail_10029.html) near travel. Other grounds/halls require their own access checks.
- **Nara National Museum's Buddhist Sculpture Hall has announced renovation closure for approximately 18 months from 14 September 2026.** That overlaps the working 2027 trips. This is a specific hall closure, not evidence the entire museum is closed; choose any museum outing from the actual open galleries and exhibitions. [Museum's hall notice](https://www.narahaku.go.jp/english/about/guide/butsuzo/). Avoid making that hall your automatic rain fallback or promising a future Shosoin exhibition date.

## A realistic first-day route

**Arrival station → Todai-ji → lunch / rest → park walk → Kasuga Taisha OR Naramachi → return train.** Use [[Japan 2027 — Nara Pocket Routes]] to choose the branch before departure.

You can pass central temple grounds or Sarusawa Pond on the approach/return when convenient, but a full paid hall visit is another activity. **Nigatsu-do, Isuien, a museum, and Wakakusa are alternatives or extensions**, not four compulsory additions. If choosing Kasuga Taisha, prioritize it before closing rather than arrive after a long shopping session. Choose an indoor meal/rest stop; carrying lunch through a deer area needs care with bags and food.

## Deer encounters and camera etiquette

Nara's deer are **wild animals**. Observe and photograph from a respectful distance; feeding is optional. If feeding, use only locally sold **shika senbei deer crackers**. Do not offer human snacks, chase or grab deer, tease them with food, or let them eat maps, paper, or plastic. Keep personal food and loose belongings secured. [Official deer guidance](https://www.visitnara.jp/venues/S01487/).

For photos, let the animal move naturally and keep the path clear. Do not stage a bowing interaction repeatedly or hold food near your face for a selfie. This is my practical photography recommendation, not a guarantee about animal behavior.

**Photo / film wishlist:** temple exterior wide shot → architectural detail where permitted → park/deer scene from a distance → lantern-path OR Naramachi street sequence → tea/sweet close-up with permission. For an overnight, consider an early park walk or a permitted Nigatsu-do exterior/viewpoint visit, subject to actual access and daylight.

**Kasuga Taisha publishes filming rules** covering equipment that disturbs worship/visitors and commercial shooting without prior permission. Consult the [shrine's photography and videography notice](https://www.kasugataisha.or.jp/wp-content/uploads/2021/07/%E6%92%AE%E5%BD%B1%E6%B3%A8%E6%84%8F%E4%BA%8B%E9%A0%85%EF%BC%88%E8%8B%B1%E8%AA%9E%EF%BC%89.pdf), and follow signs/staff instructions for each area. Do not assume all lantern interiors are open or lit for your visit. [Todai-ji's official site](https://www.todaiji.or.jp/en/) prohibits drone photography in its precincts. [Nigatsu-do's visitor page](https://www.todaiji.or.jp/en/information/nigatsudo/) prohibits photography inside the hall. For exterior viewpoints and other halls, check local tripod and worshipper restrictions before shooting.

[[Japan 2027 — Photography and Filming Wishlist|Your main filming wishlist]] has the shared permission and sequence-planning records.

## Foods and café stops

My first pairing is **a kakinoha-zushi lunch and one freshly made sweet**, with a seated tea/café break. If fish is not appealing, choose another clearly described meal rather than force a local specialty.

| Try | What it is | My suggested role |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Kakinoha-zushi** | Pressed vinegared rice with fish, wrapped in persimmon leaves | Lunch or a small tasting; check actual ingredients and storage |
| **Chagayu** | Rice porridge cooked with roasted tea | A lighter meal option if offered on your chosen menu |
| **Narazuke** | Vegetables pickled using sake lees | A small savory tasting before considering a gift |
| **Yomogi mochi** | Mugwort rice cake | One fresh sweet at Nakatanido; enjoy during the outing |
| **Kuzu-based sweets** | A regional sweet lead using kudzu starch | Compare a café or packaged product; check the label rather than assume every similarly named sweet is identical |
| **Tea / wagashi** | Tea with a Japanese sweet chosen from the menu | Your seated photography/rest break |

The [city tourism food guide](https://narashikanko.or.jp/en/narashi) explains kakinoha-zushi, chagayu, narazuke, and regional kuzu sweets. [Nakatanido's official site](https://nakatanidou.jp/) describes its fresh yomogi mochi. The [Izasa manufacturer's Nara shop guide](https://www.izasa.co.jp/en/company/) is a kakinoha-zushi browsing lead, not a guaranteed seat, item, or quoted meal.

**Nakatanido:** stop for the sweet; a mochi-pounding demonstration is a bonus only if taking place. No demonstration time is confirmed here. Avoid blocking staff or nearby pedestrian traffic for filming.

**Naramachi:** choose one café rather than a café crawl. The [official neighborhood guide](https://www.visitnara.jp/destinations/area/naramachi/) lists café leads and **Nakanishi Yosaburo**, a traditional sweet shop. Check the actual tea service, seating, opening day, and photography permission. Nara is a complement to your [[Japan Travel Guide 2027#🍵 Uji — the strongest new option for your interests|Uji tea option]], not a replacement automatically selected for the spring excursion day.

## Shopping and souvenirs for you and her

Make this a **small craft/sweet stop**, with your bigger clothing, fragrance, record, and Tamiya searches staying in their established city chapters. No specific Nara vintage inventory, her garment size, or perfume stock has been checked here.

| Idea | Why I would compare it | What to check |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **A handkerchief or small textile accessory** | A compact gift for her without a fitted-clothing decision | Material, pattern, price, and whether she would use it |
| **A patterned kaya-weave cloth** | Lightweight, useful personal/household souvenir | Actual design and intended use |
| **Packaged tea or dry sweets** | Fits your café interests or her love of sweets | Ingredients, storage, expiry beyond return, and package size |
| **A small deer-themed craft** | A local visual memory | Fragility, weight, price, and personal appeal |
| **Goshuin, if meaningful to you** | A temple/shrine visit record | Correct etiquette and the specific site's service; not a compulsory souvenir |

**Nakagawa Masashichi Shoten, Nara flagship:** the maker's guide gives **22 Ganriin-cho, Shika Saru Kitsune Building**, and introduces textiles, tea, sweets, and small crafts. Use its [official souvenir guide](https://story.nakagawa-masashichi.jp/242205) for browsing and [current flagship page](https://www.nakagawa-masashichi.jp/shop/pages/shikasarukitsune.aspx) for store information. The article's older prices and assortment are not current stock guarantees. Compare a compact textile or packaged product after seeing it; no exact embroidered design or customization lead time is promised.

For goshuin, [Todai-ji publishes a collecting guide](https://www.todaiji.or.jp/en/sutra-and-goshuin/goshuin/). Check the actual counter/service before adding it to the visit. Do not treat every hall's stamp as included in admission.

Fresh mochi or a fish/rice purchase is an **eat-during-the-trip choice** unless the seller explicitly confirms a suitable storage/transport plan. Her Nara gifts share the existing **US$200 cap**. Its relationship to the main US$300 shopping allowance remains unresolved: inside that allowance leaves up to US$100 for your other purchases; the additional-gift scenario stays within the same US$5,000 ceiling and reduces the reserve. [[Japan 2027 — Gifts for Her|Gift plan]] · [[Japan 2027 — Shopping Shortlist|Personal shopping]].

## Optional active route — Mt. Wakakusa

Compare a **paid walking entrance and an uphill viewpoint outing** with your normal park walk. [Official Wakakusa guide](https://www.visitnara.jp/venues/A00491/) describes the trails, viewpoints, and seasonal closure. The grassy mountain normally closes for part of winter; verify the specific travel-day gate hours and route before committing.

Use a walking entrance near the park, not a summit-driveway pin. Decide your turnaround point according to conditions and energy. Allow time for both ascent and descent, and descend before the confirmed gate deadline; a sunset photo is not an automatic right to stay after closing. Replace a long Naramachi/garden/shrine block with this walk, rather than append it to a full classic day. Wet ground or heat can make the ordinary park route a better choice.

No full Kasuga forest trek or Yagyu long-distance walk is scheduled. [[Japan 2027 — Hiking and Mount Fuji|Your main hiking guide]] keeps effort and Fuji decisions separate.

## Summer, November, May, and spring blossoms

| Season | My adaptation |
| --- | --- |
| **Summer** | Start the main outdoor block early, build in a seated lunch/café break, and reduce the long uphill route in heat. Consider evening events only after dated notices and return trains are checked. |
| **November** | Park/tree photography fits the autumn concept, but leaves vary by location/year. Plan major sights before their actual closing times and keep the return/daylight plan clear. |
| **Second half of May** | Prioritize greenery and a flexible park walk. Kasuga's botanical garden is an option if open; do not promise peak wisteria during those exact dates. |
| **Your provisional late-March / early-April trip** | Keep Nara on the optional-excursion day and decide from weather, energy, and actual blossom reports. A city's usual flowering period does not guarantee a particular day's blooms. |

The [official city seasonal overview](https://narashikanko.or.jp/en/narashi) describes park foliage and summer candle/lighting events. It is not a 2027 event calendar. [Kasuga's garden information](https://www.kasugataisha.or.jp/en/guidance_en/) describes spring wisteria; use current flowering reports if that becomes a priority.

**Rain alternatives:** Naramachi shops/café and a selected open temple hall; Isuien is an outdoor garden, not a fully indoor rain substitute. Its [operator calendar](https://isuien.or.jp/en/access) lists regular Tuesday closure and other maintenance periods. For a museum visit, check the specific open galleries and the renovation notice above.

## Getting here — Kyoto, Osaka, and Tokyo airports

| Start | Route to compare | Key distinction |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Kyoto Station** | Kintetsu to **Kintetsu-Nara**, direct or changing at **Yamato-Saidaiji** as your dated service requires; OR JR Nara Line to **JR Nara** | Different arrival stations and onward walking distances |
| **Osaka-Namba** | Kintetsu Nara Line to **Kintetsu-Nara**, selecting the correct direct service or change | Osaka-Namba on Kintetsu is not JR Namba; confirm your hotel-to-station route |
| **Osaka / Tennoji** | JR Yamatoji service to **JR Nara**, using the actual departure/connection | Confirm the train's destination and stops rather than board by line name alone |
| **Haneda / Narita, via Tokyo** | Airport ground transport → Tokyo-area Shinkansen station → Kyoto → selected Nara rail route | Best treated as onward travel from a Kyoto/Osaka base; Nara has no Shinkansen arrival station |
| **Kansai Airport (KIX)** | Compare airport rail via **Tennoji** to JR Nara, or a currently operating airport bus to Nara | Dated service, baggage, and onward hotel connection determine the choice |

[Official travel-to-Nara comparison](https://www.visitnara.jp/travel-to-nara/) · [Kintetsu English travel tools](https://www.kintetsu.co.jp/foreign/english/) · [JR West travel information](https://www.westjr.co.jp/global/en/) · [Local Nara Kotsu transport](https://www.narakotsu.co.jp/language/en/).

**Express and Limited Express are different products.** Kintetsu Limited Express uses reserved seats and requires a Limited Express ticket/charge **in addition to the base fare**. Ordinary Express/Rapid Express services are alternatives; compare actual journey and total price. [Operator explanation](https://www.kintetsu.co.jp/foreign/english/about/limited_express/index.html). AONIYOSHI is an optional sightseeing-train upgrade with its own availability/rules, not necessary for Nara. [Operator's AONIYOSHI page](https://www.kintetsu.co.jp/foreign/english/aoniyoshi/).

After landing at HND/NRT, keep your established Tokyo airport transfer and overnight plan unless choosing a different whole-trip route. For a day trip, go without large luggage if possible. If using Nara between Kyoto and Osaka, confirm luggage storage hours/availability and the correct arrival and departure stations first. Return to the hotel base named in the chosen route; no hotel check-in or rail reservation has been made.

## Stay length, night counts, and costs

| Alternative | Night count / effect |
| --- | --- |
| **Recommended day trip** | Tokyo 4 → Kawaguchiko 2 → Kyoto 6 → Tokyo 2 = **14 nights**. Use one Kyoto outing day for Nara; no extra hotel night. |
| **One Nara overnight** | Tokyo 4 → Kawaguchiko 2 → Kyoto 5 → Nara 1 → Tokyo 2 = **14 nights**. Replace one Kyoto night; return toward Kyoto for the onward Tokyo Shinkansen connection. |
| **Osaka plus Nara overnight** | Tokyo 4 → Kawaguchiko 2 → Kyoto 3 → Nara 1 → Osaka 2 → Tokyo 2 = **14 nights**. More hotel changes and less Kyoto time; compare before choosing. |

These alternatives do not combine automatically with Sapporo, Fukuoka, island modules, or the Fuji summit branch. The existing spring dated plan remains Tokyo 6 → Kyoto 6 → Tokyo 2, with Nara optional on 4 April.

**Current reference admission:** Todai-ji's visitor page lists **¥800 adult individual admission** for a paid hall/museum visit and **¥1,200** for the Great Buddha Hall / Todai-ji Museum joint pass; other paid halls are not thereby included. [Official admissions](https://www.todaiji.or.jp/en/information/haikan/). Kasuga Taisha lists **¥700** for special main-sanctuary worship, separate from general worship and its garden/museum tickets. [Shrine fees and access](https://www.kasugataisha.or.jp/en/about_en/basic/). These are October 2026 references, not guaranteed 2027 prices; purchase only the areas actually selected.

Requote return rail, local buses, admissions, meals, and any replaced hotel night within the existing **US$5,000 in-Japan ceiling**, excluding international flights. Nara food replaces another day's food and souvenirs share the existing shopping cap. This chapter adds no allowance or booking.

| Traditional sweets | [Nakanishi Yosaburo](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Nakanishi+Yosaburo+Naramachi+Nara) |

## Before the outing

- [ ] Choose a Kyoto/Osaka day trip or deliberately replace one hotel night.
- [ ] Choose Kasuga Taisha OR Naramachi for the first afternoon; choose the active route separately.
- [ ] Confirm both rail directions, arrival station, walking/bus connection, and last practical return.
- [ ] Check actual temple/garden hours, pagoda works, and museum gallery notices.
- [ ] Keep feeding optional; secure food, paper, and bags around deer.
- [ ] Check photography permissions, clothing fit where relevant, gift storage, and total cost.

[[Japan 2027 — Nara Pocket Routes|Open the compact route cards]] · [[Japan 2027 — Pocket 2027-04-04|Original spring excursion-choice card]]

# 🦌 Nara — pocket routes

[[Japan 2027 — Nara Chapter|← Full chapter, official sources, transport, and Google Maps]] · [[Japan 2027 — Seasonal Planning and Bookings|Budget / bookings]]

**Undated alternatives:** choose one card for a day trip. They are not three consecutive scheduled days. One overnight can split the classic park visit and Naramachi/garden outing across two days, adapting the second day to onward travel. The original 4 April card still offers Uji, Nara, Osaka, or more Kyoto.

## Card 1 — classic Nara, with one afternoon choice

**Anchor:** selected JR Nara or Kintetsu-Nara arrival → Todai-ji → park → Kasuga Taisha OR Naramachi → return.

| Block | Plan |
| --- | --- |
| Arrival | Confirm station, onward walk/bus, and return train. |
| Morning | Todai-ji / Great Buddha Hall; optional Nigatsu-do only if steps, time, and energy suit. |
| Midday | Lunch and seated break; keep personal food and paper secured around deer. |
| Afternoon A | Kasuga Taisha approach and permitted worship areas; visit before actual closing. |
| Afternoon B | Naramachi streets, one café/sweet, and a compact craft browse. |
| Return | Selected station → Kyoto or Osaka hotel base. Do not assume the two Nara stations are interchangeable. |

**Food:** kakinoha-zushi OR another suitable lunch, plus one sweet. **Shot:** temple exterior → permitted detail → distant deer/park scene → lantern path OR street/tea scene. **Buy:** one small textile or packaged gift, optional. **Fallback:** shorten the park, choose an open hall/café, and return earlier if conditions require.

- [ ] Choose A or B; every optional garden/museum is another activity.
- [ ] Temple fees/closing and actual return checked.
- [ ] Feeding optional; only deer crackers if chosen. Follow shrine/temple filming rules.

## Card 2 — Naramachi, crafts, sweets, and a selected garden

**Anchor:** central Nara → Sarusawa Pond / Naramachi → one paid garden or cultural stop → café / return.

| Block | Plan |
| --- | --- |
| Morning | Sarusawa area and Naramachi streets; Gangoji OR Koshi-no-Ie if open and appealing. |
| Lunch / break | A clearly described meal, tea, and one sweet; check actual café service. |
| Afternoon choice | Isuien if open and the weather suits, OR a selected central craft-shopping block. |
| Gift browse | Nakagawa Masashichi Shoten flagship or another chosen shop; cap the number of purchases. |

**Food:** chagayu if offered, or a normal café lunch; Nakatanido fresh mochi is an optional convenient stop, with no pounding demonstration promised. **Shot:** street layers → textile/shop detail with permission → sweet close-up → garden composition if chosen. **Buy:** handkerchief, kaya-weave cloth, packaged tea, OR a dry sweet.

**Photo adjustment:** pagoda restoration can obstruct the traditional Kohfukuji/Sarusawa shot. **Rain adjustment:** cafés/shops/open halls; Isuien is an outdoor garden. A museum option requires checking its actual open galleries because the Buddhist Sculpture Hall's announced renovation overlaps 2027.

- [ ] Isuien's closure calendar, shop hours, and seating options checked.
- [ ] No specific pagoda view, product stock, customization, or gallery assumed.
- [ ] Ingredients, expiry, storage, and existing gift/shopping cap checked.

## Card 3 — park and Wakakusa, if the walking route is open

**Anchor:** selected station → park / optional short temple stop → paid Wakakusa walking entrance → viewpoint / descent → meal and return.

**Before leaving:** confirm seasonal opening, the walking entrance, gate deadline, weather, and outward/return transport. Use the walking gate, not a summit car-access pin. Set a comfortable turnaround point and allow descent time.

**Main block:** a limited park/temple visit plus the uphill outing; omit the long shrine/garden/Naramachi circuit. Take a seated meal/rest afterward. A full ascent is optional; choose an ordinary park walk instead when the effort or conditions do not suit.

**Shot:** walking transition → grass/hill detail → city panorama where permitted. **Buy:** none required. **Sunset:** only if verified opening/access and return arrangements support it. **Summer:** reduce uphill effort in heat. **November:** use actual daylight and descend before the gate closes. **Rain fallback:** another card with shorter outdoor blocks.

- [ ] Walking effort and a realistic turnaround selected.
- [ ] Open gate, path conditions, daylight, and descent deadline checked.
- [ ] No full forest trek, summit drive, or guided outing booked.

**Overnight choice:** one Nara night replaces another hotel night. Confirm luggage storage/check-in and second-day onward travel first. An early park walk is optional; do not turn departure morning into another full outing without time for transport.

# 🍁🌿 Japan — November versus May

**Nagoya comparison:** [[Japan 2027 — Nagoya Chapter]] adapts Tokugawaen and city/town walks for November color versus May greenery without assuming peak dates.

**Okinawa comparison:** [[Japan 2027 — Okinawa Chapter]] distinguishes November land/coast exploring from late-May rainy-season planning; neither guarantees beach conditions.

**Nara comparison:** [[Japan 2027 — Nara Chapter]] adapts a Kyoto/Osaka excursion to November colors or May greenery; late-May wisteria and annual peaks are not guaranteed.

**Sapporo comparison:** [[Japan 2027 — Sapporo Chapter]] treats late May as Hokkaido spring and mid/late November as a colder city-food option; flowering and foliage need local dated checks.

**Fukuoka comparison:** [[Japan 2027 — Fukuoka Chapter]] adapts city walks, food, and a Dazaifu/coastal outing to November or May without assuming annual conditions or events.

**Island scenery comparison:** [[Japan 2027 — Best Islands]] adds November foliage and May greenery choices, with suggested stays and explicit mainland-night tradeoffs.

**Osaka comparison:** [[Japan 2027 — Osaka Chapter]] includes Minoh for November foliage when reports support it, or May greenery, alongside the same city shopping and food options. Osaka is an optional route change in either season.

[[Japan 2027 — Seasonal Guides|← Seasonal guides]] · [[Japan 2027 — Autumn Guide|November route]] · [[Japan 2027 — Seasonal Planning and Bookings|Budget and decisions]]

**Purpose:** choose between November autumn colors and May greenery/warmer days after reviewing summer. Working year 2027; no dates selected. My comparison uses the same **15 days / 14 nights** and **Tokyo 4 → Fuji lakes 2 → Kyoto 6 → Tokyo 2** route to isolate seasonal differences.

| Dimension | November | May, after Golden Week |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Main scenery | Autumn color in Kyoto/Tokyo; exact timing varies | Fresh greenery, green maples, and other spring flowers; not a Tokyo/Kyoto blossom trip |
| Walking feel | Cooler days and colder evenings; plan earlier hike finishes | Generally warmer conditions for city walks; heat/rain remain possible |
| Photography | My pick for leaves, temple contrasts, reflections, and possible evening light-ups | My pick for green forest scenes, gardens, and brighter spring landscapes |
| Hiking | Low-elevation options; high mountains may have winter conditions | Low-elevation options; verify high-mountain conditions rather than treating May as summer |
| Fuji | Lake/viewpoint visit; summit climb outside normal season | Lake/viewpoint visit; summit climb outside normal season |
| Crowds | Popular foliage locations can be busy; compare real lodging quotes | Golden Week is especially busy; compare the calmer second half of May |
| Clothing/shopping | Browse outerwear and layers if useful at home | Browse lighter pieces if useful at home; exact seasonal stock varies |
| What to verify | Foliage progress, night-visit announcements, daylight, trail access | Holiday dates, garden/flower conditions, rain forecasts, trail access |

[JNTO November](https://www.japan.travel/en/guide/november/) · [JNTO May](https://www.japan.travel/en/guide/may/) · [National parks seasons and climate](https://faq.japan-travel.jnto.jp/national-parks/plan-your-visit/seasons-and-climate/) · [Fuji season](https://www.fujisan-climb.jp/en/season/).

May's second half is my starting window for warmer walking and greenery. JNTO notes that Tokyo/Kyoto's cherry blossoms have generally finished by May; later northern blossom travel requires a different regional route. Neither season guarantees sun or a clear Fuji view, and no claim that either is always cheaper is supported without dated quotes.

## May adaptation of the autumn itinerary

Keep the same hotels/base count provisionally and make these changes:

| Days | May version |
| --- | --- |
| 1–4 | Tokyo arrival, Asakusa, Kichijoji, and Takao or a garden; photograph greenery rather than chasing leaf color. |
| 5–6 | Fuji lakes, choosing two manageable viewpoints and keeping weather flexibility. |
| 7 | Kyoto transfer; rest and easy dinner. |
| 8 | Nanzen-ji/Eikando area for green maples and architecture; no autumn illumination assumed. |
| 9 | Arashiyama river/greenery and one chosen temple. |
| 10 | Tofuku-ji for green-maple scenery, or a lighter central Kyoto day. |
| 11 | Uji matcha/café outing. |
| 12 | Daimonji OR Kurama–Kibune if conditions suit; a city fallback remains available. |
| 13–15 | Tokyo return, Tamiya plus selected gifts/clothing, packing, departure. |

Kyoto confirms that its well-known autumn maple sites are also green-maple destinations. [Official green-maple suggestions](https://global.kyoto.travel/en/faq/detail.php?faq_id=1034). Admission, opening days, and trail access require the actual May dates.

## How I would choose for you

- **November:** first choice if the trip centers on seasonal photography and Kyoto temple scenery.
- **May:** first choice if warmer walking days and greenery matter more than autumn color.
- **Summer:** the season to investigate a Fuji summit climb, plus festival possibilities, while adapting city days to heat.

These are personal recommendations based on your interests. All three retain cafés, matcha, vintage clothes, records, Tamiya, and her gifts; those interests do not require a particular season.

## Decision record

Preferred season: … · year: … · arrival/departure: … · main scenery goal: … · preferred hike effort: … · Fuji scenic visit/summit: …

Before choosing, compare full hotel/transport quotes for the same night count, flight arrival/departure dates, and cancellation terms. Save the result in the seasonal planning note. The summer, November, and May concepts are alternatives; their budgets are not added together.

# 🏝️ Japan 2027 — Okinawa chapter

**Klook tours to compare:** [[Japan 2027 — Klook More Cities and Islands#Okinawa and Kerama|Main-island and Kerama options]]

**Next city chapter:** [[Japan 2027 — Nagoya Chapter|Nagoya]] — a mainland rail-stop comparison with food, city sights, shopping, and optional Inuyama/Ghibli branches.

[[Japan 2027 — Seasonal Guides|← Seasonal guides]] · [[Japan 2027 — Okinawa Pocket Routes|Four pocket routes]] · [[Japan 2027 — Best Islands|Compare islands]] · [[Japan 2027 — Seasonal Planning and Bookings|Budget / bookings]]

[[#Google Maps — places and transport|📍 Google Maps]] · [[#Foods to try|🍜 Food]] · [[#Getting here — Haneda Narita and Kansai|🛬 Getting here]]

**Research checked:** 7 October 2026. **Working year:** 2027, unconfirmed. This is an optional southern-island chapter for your photography, food, shopping, gifts, and outdoor interests. Dates, hotels, flights, ferries, tours, marine activities, and product stock remain unconfirmed.

## Which Okinawa do we mean?

**This chapter starts with Okinawa Main Island**, flying into **Naha Airport (OKA)**. Naha, Shuri, Chatan, Onna, and Motobu are places on this island. The **Kerama Islands**, including Zamami, are separate islands reached by boat from Naha.

**Ishigaki / Taketomi and Miyako are different regional choices**, already covered in [[Japan 2027 — Best Islands]]. They are not neighborhoods of Naha or automatic day trips from an Onna hotel. Compare one region first; adding inter-island flights and hotel changes requires a fresh route. [Official Okinawa overview](https://visitokinawajapan.com/discover/okinawa-at-a-glance/) · [Ishigaki](https://visitokinawajapan.com/destinations/yaeyama-islands/ishigaki-island/) · [Miyako Islands](https://visitokinawajapan.com/destinations/miyako-islands/).

## My starting recommendation for you

Compare **four nights based in Naha**: arrival and an easy local meal → Shuri / city culture and crafts → one Chatan coastal outing → **Zamami day trip OR a Motobu / Churaumi Aquarium day** → departure. Four nights provide three full outing days. Swap the middle days when conditions and reservations justify it.

This is my simplest first comparison while your driving preference remains open. **A city hotel is not a beachfront resort.** If beach time matters more than city browsing, compare **five nights split Naha 2 + Onna 3**, with transfers, resort location, and transport priced first. Do not use the Naha day cards as if every bus/port were equally convenient from Onna.

With only three nights, keep city culture and one coast/boat/north choice. A longer stay can add slower beach days; it does not need an outing to every named area.

## Choose your base and transport

| Base | Best reason to choose it | Transport implications |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Naha near Yui Rail / Kokusai Street** | Airport access, meals, crafts, Shuri, and a Tomari-port outing | Strongest starting comparison without a car. Confirm the walk to hotel and port; monorail does not reach Onna or Motobu. |
| **Chatan** | Waterfront cafés, sunset walks, American Village shopping | Compare dated buses/airport transfer and the actual hotel location. A coastal base still needs a northern/port transport plan. |
| **Onna coast** | Beach/resort stay and coastal photography | Compare hotel shuttle/bus stops, meals, and beach access, or a deliberate rental-car plan. Do not assume every resort is walkable from every stop. |
| **Motobu area** | More time around Churaumi / Ocean Expo Park | An alternative northern base for a longer visit; it is farther from Naha activities and airport transfers. |

[Naha guide](https://visitokinawajapan.com/destinations/okinawa-main-island/southern-okinawa-main-island/naha/) · [Chatan guide](https://visitokinawajapan.com/destinations/okinawa-main-island/central-okinawa-main-island/chatan/) · [Onna coast guide](https://visitokinawajapan.com/destinations/okinawa-main-island/northern-okinawa-main-island/onna-coast/) · [Motobu guide](https://visitokinawajapan.com/destinations/okinawa-main-island/northern-okinawa-main-island/motobu-peninsula/).

**Without a car:** use Yui Rail / walking for selected Naha sights, a dated bus/shuttle for a chosen coast destination, and a reserved tour only if its stops, pickup, time, and price suit. A taxi is a priced alternative, not an assumed unlimited transport budget. Select one destination block per excursion; a bus map does not establish that a multi-stop driving itinerary works by bus. [Official getting-around guide](https://visitokinawajapan.com/plan-your-trip/getting-around-okinawa/) · [Yui Rail operator](https://www.yui-rail.co.jp/en/).

**With a car:** compare the full rental quote, pickup/return travel, insurance terms, fuel, parking, and any tolls. Before choosing this branch, confirm the driver's accepted documents and eligibility with the rental operator using the [official rental-vehicle guidance](https://visitokinawajapan.com/plan-your-trip/getting-around-okinawa/rental-vehicles-in-okinawa/). A foreign license or a document called an international permit does not by itself confirm acceptance. No car or driving eligibility has been assumed.

## Naha — culture, food, and crafts

**Shuri Castle Park:** choose a park / heritage visit and check the actual areas available on your date. The operator announces the restored **Seiden main hall interior opening for 23 November 2026**, with designated date/time reservations for interior viewing. A paid-area ticket **without** that reservation excludes the main hall interior. The announced adult individual fee from that date is **¥1,000**, with no extra charge for the timed designation. [Official opening and ticket guide](https://oki-park.jp/shurijo/en/ticket_guide/).

Treat this as an announced change checked in October 2026, not proof that a particular 2027 slot is available. Use [current park notices](https://oki-park.jp/shurijo/en/) and [operator access instructions](https://oki-park.jp/shurijo/access) to coordinate your monorail/bus/walk and ticket. Avoid relying on pre-fire photos or older restoration-tour assumptions for what you will see.

**Kokusai Street / Makishi area:** a manageable meal/shopping block, with the market as a browsing lead. Compare a clearly priced menu rather than assume a seafood purchase includes preparation or upstairs dining. **Tsuboya Yachimun Street:** pottery browsing and a café alternative. [Naha visitor guide](https://visitokinawajapan.com/destinations/okinawa-main-island/southern-okinawa-main-island/naha/).

**Café lead for you:** the official Naha feature lists **Okinawan cafe BUKU-BUKU** in Tsuboya and other café/gallery stops. Choose one seated break and check the actual menu, opening day, and filming permission. [Official café / craft walking feature](https://visitokinawajapan.com/travel-inspiration/get-to-know-naha/). A named café is a lead, not a booked seat or guaranteed drink.

## Coastal exploring — choose a manageable block

**Chatan / American Village:** waterfront walk, a café/meal, and a limited browsing block. My main photo goal here is **late-afternoon coast → sunset if visible → illuminated street detail**, with a confirmed return journey. Depot Island / Sunset Walk and the Miyagi Coast are separate possible blocks; choose by your transport and pace. [Official Chatan guide](https://visitokinawajapan.com/destinations/okinawa-main-island/central-okinawa-main-island/chatan/).

**Onna:** compare a beach-oriented stay or one selected coastal destination. Longer transfer time and resort access matter more than the number of beach pins. A paid guided snorkeling activity is an optional separate purchase after checking operator location, equipment, suitability, and weather. [Official Onna overview](https://visitokinawajapan.com/destinations/okinawa-main-island/northern-okinawa-main-island/onna-coast/).

**Motobu / Churaumi Aquarium:** dedicate an outing day to the aquarium / nearby Ocean Expo Park. The operator describes a substantial journey from Naha Airport, approximately **two hours by car** or **two and a half hours by express-bus options** before allowing for your actual connection and traffic. This is an orientation estimate, not a guaranteed hotel-to-door journey. [Aquarium access](https://churaumi.okinawa/guide/access/) · [Park's bus information and operator links](https://oki-park.jp/kaiyohaku/acc/147).

For the car-free version, compare a through bus/shuttle or a suitable dated tour, confirm the actual stop and walk to the entrance, and save the return. Do not combine a full aquarium visit, Onna beach day, Chatan sunset, and a Kerama trip in one day. [Aquarium visitor guide](https://churaumi.okinawa/en/). Kouri or other northern detours would require a separate, workable transport plan; they have not been added automatically.

## Kerama option — Zamami day trip from Naha

**Route:** Naha hotel → **Tomari Port** → the correct reserved boat to **Zamami Port** → selected beach/village area using confirmed island transport → boat back → hotel. The high-speed boat and larger ferry use different boarding arrangements; confirm your vessel and pier. [Tourism association access guide](https://www.zamamitourism.com/en/transport).

Choose **one beach/viewpoint or a guided marine activity** after coordinating its start/finish with both sailings. **Furuzamami Beach** is a sightseeing/swimming lead subject to its actual open zones, supervision, weather, and local transfer. A sea-view photo day does not require entering the water. [Official Zamami island guide](https://visitokinawajapan.com/destinations/kerama-islands/zamami-island/).

Book/check **both directions** rather than assume a return seat. Boats can sell out or be canceled by wind/waves, and the operator determines daily service. The tourism association supplies information; **boat reservations are managed by the village office**, not the association. [Reservation guidance](https://www.zamamitourism.com/en/aboutboat-1) · [Village office for current sailing status and booking links](https://www.vill.zamami.okinawa.jp/).

Do not schedule this on your flight day. For an island overnight, replace a Naha hotel night and reassess weather/luggage/return buffers first; this chapter does not include that overnight. Do not add Aka and Tokashiki transfers to the same introductory day. If boat conditions fail, use a land day or the northern option when its own transport is workable.

### Beach and marine-day checks

Check whether swimming is allowed, the managed swimming zone, supervision, weather/sea conditions, and operator guidance. Stay out when local restrictions or conditions require; use appropriate flotation/equipment for chosen activities. Protect coral and follow local instructions. [Official sea-activity guidance](https://visitokinawajapan.com/wp-content/themes/visit-okinawa_multi-language/lang/en/assets/document/plan-your-trip/useful-information/303/All%20About%20the%20Sea%20in%20Okinawa.pdf).

No guaranteed calm sea, turtle sighting, diving qualification, snorkeling booking, or beach opening is assumed. Your default outdoor activity remains walking and photography until a water activity is deliberately selected.

## Foods to try

My first choices are **Okinawa soba**, **goya champuru**, and one **beni-imo or sata-andagi sweet**. Add a pork dish or taco rice on another day rather than order every specialty at once.

| Food | What to expect | Suggested role |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Okinawa soba / soki soba** | Local noodle soup; pork-rib topping is a common soki version | Arrival meal or city lunch; inspect the actual broth/toppings |
| **Goya champuru** | Bitter-melon stir-fry, often with tofu and other ingredients | A main dish or shared side; actual recipes vary |
| **Rafute** | Slow-cooked pork dish | One dinner if pork appeals |
| **Taco rice** | Rice with taco-style toppings | Casual lunch; an alternative to another noodle meal |
| **Sata andagi** | Fried doughnut-style sweet | One fresh snack, not a required souvenir box |
| **Beni-imo tart** | Purple sweet-potato sweet | Taste one, then compare a small gift box |

[Official Okinawan food culture](https://visitokinawajapan.com/discover/food-and-longevity/okinawan-food-culture/) · [JNTO cuisine examples](https://www.japan.travel/en/gastronomy/article-indigenous-cuisine-on-the-menu/) · [Official street-food guide](https://visitokinawajapan.com/travel-inspiration/must-try-okinawa-street-foods/) · [Okashigoten's sweet guide](https://okashigoten.co.jp/en/faq/).

For an izakaya evening, start with the Naha/Kokusai area near your chosen hotel and compare actual menu, seating, cover charge if any, and total price. It is an area lead rather than a verified individual-restaurant reservation. Keep coastal sunset and a long dinner realistic against the final bus/transport plan.

## Clothing, crafts, and gifts

| Buy idea | Best match | Where / source |

No exact print, price, or size stock confirmed. |
| **Small bingata textile/accessory** | For her: a compact fashion-related gift without a fitted-garment decision | Compare origin, pattern, material, and price; [official bingata craft guide](https://visitokinawajapan.com/travel-inspiration/ryukyu-bingata-okinawa-dyeing/). |
| **Yachimun pottery / small shisa** | For you or her: one craft keepsake | Browse Tsuboya; inspect packing, fragility, maker, and actual cost. [Naha craft/café guide](https://visitokinawajapan.com/travel-inspiration/get-to-know-naha/) · [Traditional craft experiences](https://visitokinawajapan.com/travel-inspiration/must-try-traditional-experiences/). |

Your **L tops / EU 44–46 trousers** are references; use actual measurements or try on before buying. Her clothing size and perfume preferences remain unknown. No current Okinawa Y2K/Korean-style product stock or perfume counter has been verified here; the existing [[Japan 2027 — Gifts for Her|gift shortlist]] remains the place to compare those options.

For sweets, check the **specific product's** ingredients, expiry, and storage. Packaged original tarts and chilled/frozen fresh sweets are different products; do not assume identical travel suitability. A pottery workshop also needs pickup/shipping timing before treating the finished item as a same-day souvenir.

Choose one keeper or one small gift within the existing allowance. Her purchases share **US$200**, with inclusion in the **US$300 shopping/miscellaneous** allowance still open. If included, up to US$100 remains for your other shopping; if additional inside the same trip ceiling, the previously described reserve decreases. No new Okinawa allowance is added. [[Japan 2027 — Shopping Shortlist|Shopping priorities]].

## Photography and filming wishlist

**My sequence:** Naha street establishing shot → permitted Shuri architecture/detail → Tsuboya craft texture with permission → coast/water wide shot → food close-up → sunset or night street. Choose one coast scene you can reach comfortably; a sunny brochure image is not a forecast.

Ask before filming in shops, cafés, museums, or guided activities and follow castle/site restrictions. Keep tripods out of busy walkways and do not film other customers without consent. Water filming is an optional equipment/activity decision, not a reason to enter an unsuitable area. [[Japan 2027 — Photography and Filming Wishlist|Main shot and permission records]].

## Compare your seasons

| Season | Main fit | Adaptation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Summer / July–early August** | Beach scenery and a possible marine activity | Heat and typhoons can affect plans; keep flexible land days and mainland return nights. A late-July/early-August trip is not outside typhoon season. |
| **November** | City culture, food, coastal walks, and a break from mainland cold | A good comparison for land-based exploring. Confirm each beach's swimming permission and conditions; it is not a Kyoto-style autumn-color module. |
| **Second half of May** | Warm southern scenery, crafts, and food | Usually overlaps the rainy season; retain indoor/short land alternatives rather than assume clear beach days. |
| **Late March / early April** | Another climate/culture option | Separate from your Kyoto/Tokyo blossom route; it requires replaced nights and a whole-trip revision. |

[Official climate / seasons guide](https://visitokinawajapan.com/plan-your-trip/when-to-visit-climate-seasons/) · [May guidance](https://visitokinawajapan.com/plan-your-trip/when-to-visit-climate-seasons/okinawa-may/) · [November guidance](https://visitokinawajapan.com/plan-your-trip/when-to-visit-climate-seasons/okinawa-november/). The [official FAQ](https://visitokinawajapan.com/plan-your-trip/faq/) places the typical rainy season in May–June and says typhoons are most common in August/September, with possible occurrences June–October. These are general patterns, not 2027 forecasts or guarantees outside those months.

## Getting here — Haneda, Narita, and Kansai

| Start | Route to compare | Before selecting |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Haneda (HND)** | HND → **Naha (OKA)** domestic flight → hotel transfer | Dated fare, baggage, terminal/check-in, and international-to-domestic connection rules |
| **Narita (NRT)** | A dated direct NRT → OKA flight, if suitable | Airline/terminal and full fare with baggage; do not assume changing to HND is required |
| **Kyoto / Osaka** | Hotel → selected Kansai airport, such as ITM or KIX → OKA | Compare each exact airport and hotel-to-airport journey; these airports are not interchangeable |
| **Okinawa → Tokyo** | Hotel → OKA → chosen Tokyo airport → final Tokyo hotel | Retain the Tokyo buffer nights before international departure |
| **International arrival at OKA** | Arrival formalities → selected monorail/bus/taxi transfer | Correct terminal exit, last usable connection, and hotel location |

[JAL route / timetable tools](https://www.jal.co.jp/jp/en/dom/route/index.html) · [Peach routes / schedules](https://www.flypeach.com/en/lm/st/routemap) · [Naha Airport terminal and transport information](https://www.naha-airport.co.jp/en/). These links do not establish a particular 2027 service or fare. Use **OKA** for this module; ISG, MMY, and SHI serve different island-region choices in [[Japan 2027 — Island Access from Haneda and Narita]].

**Airport → Naha:** compare **Yui Rail** to the station nearest your hotel, followed by the actual walk/taxi. **Airport → Chatan/Onna:** compare a dated bus/shuttle, prearranged transfer, or deliberate rental-car pickup; Yui Rail does not complete that coastal journey. **Hotel → Tomari Port:** verify your boat's terminal/pier and check-in deadline, separately from the airport transfer. [Yui Rail operator](https://www.yui-rail.co.jp/en/) · [Zamami access](https://www.zamamitourism.com/en/transport).

## Fit it into 15 days / 14 nights

| Alternative | Nights | What it replaces |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Four-night main-island module** | Tokyo 4 → Kawaguchiko 2 → Kyoto 2 → Okinawa 4 → Tokyo 2 = **14** | Four Kyoto nights; only one full non-transfer Kyoto day remains |
| **Five-night beach/culture preference** | Tokyo 4 → Kawaguchiko 2 → Kyoto 1 → Naha 2 → Onna 3 → Tokyo 2 = **14** | Kyoto becomes a brief stop; compare whether that transfer is worthwhile |
| **Okinawa-focused alternative** | Tokyo 4 → Kawaguchiko 2 → Okinawa 6 → Tokyo 2 = **14** | Omits Kyoto/Osaka; allows a slower main-island stay with bases chosen deliberately |

These are alternatives to the existing seasonal outlines and to Ishigaki/Miyako, Sapporo, and Fukuoka modules. They do not modify the dated spring cards. The Fuji scenic/summit choice remains separate. For the five-night version, arrival/transfer days reduce full beach time; Naha 2 + Onna 3 is five nights, not five full sightseeing days.

Keep the existing **US$5,000 in-Japan ceiling**, excluding international flights. Requote domestic flights with baggage, airport travel, replaced accommodation, ferry return tickets, any resort transfers/car costs, guided activities, meals, and admissions. The seasonal **US$400 intercity planning allocation** does not prove these flights/ferries fit. Count transport legs once and preserve the final mainland nights; this buffer reduces connection pressure but cannot guarantee disruption-free travel. No tickets or lodging have been booked.

# 🏝️ Okinawa — pocket routes

[[Japan 2027 — Okinawa Chapter|← Full chapter, official sources, airport access, and Google Maps]] · [[Japan 2027 — Seasonal Planning and Bookings|Budget / bookings]]

**Undated Naha-base cards:** four nights give arrival, three full outing days, and departure. Use Cards 1–4 with Card 4's A OR B choice. Swap Cards 2–4 when conditions/reservations justify. For three nights, omit one full outing. An Onna split-base version needs different transfers; these are not four consecutive days of driving around every region.

## Card 1 — arrive and settle into Naha

**Route:** Naha Airport (OKA) → selected Yui Rail / bus / taxi connection → hotel. Confirm baggage and check-in arrangements first.

**If time and energy allow:** short Kokusai/hotel-area walk → Okinawa soba or a nearby meal → rest. Arrival is a transport day, not a ferry-and-beach day. For a late arrival, keep the meal near the hotel and omit shopping.

**Shot:** city arrival transition → street scene → first food detail with permission. **Buy:** none required. **Check:** actual arrival terminal, final usable connection, and next day's weather/reservation.

## Card 2 — Shuri, a seated meal, and Naha crafts

| Block | Plan |
| --- | --- |
| Morning | Confirmed monorail/bus/walk → Shuri Castle Park. Interior only with the correct available timed reservation. |
| Midday | Lunch/rest; choose goya champuru, soba, or another suitable menu. |
| Afternoon | Tsuboya pottery street plus one café OR Makishi/Kokusai browsing. Reduce the block if the castle visit runs long. |
| Evening | A nearby dinner; rafute is optional if pork appeals. |

**Shot:** permitted castle architecture → street/craft texture → tea/sweet detail. **Buy:** kariyushi shirt after fit check OR compact craft / packaged sweet. Use current MANGO HOUSE branch information before navigating to a shop. Her size and fragrance preferences remain open.

**Rain alternative:** selected café/covered shopping and any confirmed indoor part of the chosen visit; outdoor castle paths still need weather judgment. **Check:** ticket type, actual opening, transport, shop hours, filming permission, and shared shopping cap.

## Card 3 — Chatan waterfront and a realistic sunset plan

**Route:** Naha hotel → dated coast bus/shuttle, priced taxi, or a deliberately selected rental-car route → Chatan → confirmed return. Yui Rail alone does not complete this journey.

**Main block:** American Village / Depot Island waterfront → lunch or café → limited shopping → late-afternoon photos. Miyagi Coast is an alternative or a separately routed extension, not a compulsory extra walk. Choose one main coastal block.

**Food:** taco rice or a clearly priced café meal, plus one sweet if wanted. **Shot:** coast wide → street color → food → sunset if visible. **Buy:** none required; one shirt/accessory replaces another planned shopping choice.

**Return:** verify the last workable bus before committing to sunset/dinner. A later taxi needs a realistic quote. **Bad-weather alternative:** Naha cafés/crafts and a shorter outdoor walk. For an Onna hotel base, rebuild this day's start/finish and avoid an unnecessary Naha return.

## Card 4 — choose A OR B

### A. Zamami — one Kerama island day

**Route:** Naha hotel → correct Tomari Port pier → reserved boat to Zamami Port → confirmed local beach/viewpoint or activity transfer → reserved boat back → Naha hotel.

**Main block:** one Furuzamami/beach-view area OR one guided marine activity that finishes in time for the return. Choose lunch/break and island transport before departure. The beach pin does not confirm swimming permission or supervision. Observe local sea rules; land photos are a valid option.

**Shot:** port transition → sea/coast wide → village detail where appropriate. **Buy:** none required. **Check:** both seats, actual sailing status, pier/check-in, local return, and the activity's equipment/conditions. Boats can cancel by wind/waves; use a land fallback. No Aka/Tokashiki hopping or island overnight is counted.

### B. Motobu — Churaumi Aquarium / Ocean Expo Park

**Route:** selected dated through bus/shuttle/tour or rental-car route → confirmed aquarium-area stop/entrance → return. Allow a full excursion day; the northern journey is substantial.

**Main block:** aquarium visit → meal/break → a manageable nearby park block if conditions and return time suit. Check current admission, opening, and any program notices. Do not add a full Onna beach day and Chatan sunset afterward.

**Shot:** exterior/park scene → permitted exhibit detail → coast context. **Buy:** one compact keepsake only if wanted. **Check:** outward/return transport, stop-to-entrance walking, pickup if using a tour, and total cost. This is an alternative to the boat day, not a guaranteed transport option during severe weather.

**Departure day:** hotel → OKA with baggage/check-in buffer. If renting, allow return/refueling and transfer time. No ferry outing or northern day trip is scheduled before the flight. The Tokyo-return alternatives retain two separate Tokyo hotel nights before international departure.

# 🐙 Japan 2027 — Osaka chapter

**Klook tours to compare:** [[Japan 2027 — Klook More Cities and Islands#Osaka|Osaka tour and experience options]]

**Compare a mainland stop:** [[Japan 2027 — Nagoya Chapter|Nagoya food, Osu/Sakae, and two-night routes]]. Choose which city nights to replace before combining modules.

**Nearby culture day:** [[Japan 2027 — Nara Chapter|Nara]] — compare Kintetsu from Osaka-Namba or JR to Nara, with a separate full-day route and optional replaced overnight.

**Next city to compare:** [[Japan 2027 — Fukuoka Chapter|Fukuoka]] — rail onward to Hakata, three-night food/culture option, and explicit route changes.

**Continue west / island alternatives:** [[Japan 2027 — Best Islands]] includes Miyajima and Naoshima modules and a 14-night Miyajima-plus-Osaka concept with KIX departure, subject to flight comparison.

[[Japan 2027 — Seasonal Guides|← Seasonal guides]] · [[Japan 2027 — Osaka Pocket Routes|Phone-ready routes]] · [[Japan 2027 — Seasonal Planning and Bookings|Budget and reservations]]

**Built:** 7 October 2026. **Status:** optional chapter for your summer, November, or May trip; no dates or bookings assigned. The inherited planning year is 2027. Shop information below was checked against official sources today; future hours, exact products, sizes, prices, and branch stock remain unconfirmed.

## My starting recommendation for you

Choose **two Osaka nights** if food, fashion, hobby shops, cafés, records, and night photography deserve their own chapter. Start with Minami, then use the second day for Kita or Minoh. If Kyoto temples and matcha matter more, keep your Kyoto base and make Osaka one day trip instead.

Your best matches are **Den Den Town for models**, **Amerikamura/Shinsaibashi for clothes**, **Dotonbori for food and neon**, and **Nakazakicho/Umeda for a slower café-and-record outing**. Minoh supplies the nature alternative. These priorities are my selection for your interests, rather than a promise that every shop stocks your wishlist.

## Understand the city in three blocks

| Block | What to explore | How I would use it |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Minami / south | Namba, Nipponbashi/Den Den Town, Shinsaibashi, Amerikamura, Dotonbori | Main shopping-and-food day; finish with evening photos |
| Kita / north | Osaka/Umeda, Nakazakicho, Doyamacho | Café, fashion, and one record shop; slower second day |
| Minoh / north of the city | Station-to-waterfall walk | Separate daylight outing, replacing some city browsing |

[Official Amerikamura guide](https://osaka-info.jp/en/spot/america-mura-american-village/) describes its vintage-clothing and music culture. [Den Den Town](https://osaka-info.jp/en/discover-kansai/osaka-den-den-town/) is the electronics/pop-culture browsing area. [Nakazakicho](https://osaka-info.jp/en/special/dc/event/detail637/) offers a retro streetscape. Explore each as an area; individual stores set their own opening and filming rules.

## Shopping shortlist — what I would look for

| Stop / area | Your shopping idea | Location / current source |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Joshin Super Kids Land Honten, Den Den Town | One compact plastic model or RC-related item; ask whether the exact Tamiya kit or Mini 4WD item you want is stocked | **4-12-4 Nipponbashi, Naniwa-ku**; official hours currently 10:00–20:00. [Joshin branch](https://store.joshin.co.jp/detail/1746/) |
| Amerikamura | For you: graphic tee, denim jacket, flannel, or trousers; browse condition and measurements before deciding | West of Shinsaibashi around Triangle Park. [Area guide](https://osaka-info.jp/en/spot/america-mura-american-village/) |
| WEGO Shinsaibashi | For her: browse a Y2K-inspired top, small bag, or accessory; for you: casual tee or layer if the fit suits | **Shinsaibashi GATE 4–5F, 2-5-5 Shinsaibashisuji, Chuo-ku**. [Shopping-street branch listing](https://www.shinsaibashi.or.jp/stores/show/1/) |
| SPINNS HEP FIVE, Kita | Alternative clothing browse for her: cardigan/cami, streetwear, or an accessory, depending on the collection | **HEP FIVE 2F, 5-15 Kakudacho, Kita-ku**. [Official store directory](https://www.spinns.jp/f/shop) |
| DiskUnion Osaka, Kita | One used record or CD that you actually want; inspect condition and consider luggage space | **ACT III 1F, 15-17 Doyamacho, Kita-ku**. [Official branch](https://diskunion.net/shop/osaka) |
| LiLo Coffee Roasters, Minami | Seated coffee break if seating is available; consider beans as an optional gift after checking roast date and luggage space | **1F, 1-10-28 Nishishinsaibashi, Chuo-ku**. [Official locations](https://coffee.liloinveve.com/pages/store-location) |

Joshin's branch explicitly lists plastic models, RC models, railway models, and Gunpla; exact Tamiya inventory needs a separate check. Your Tokyo **Tamiya Shinbashi** visit remains the existing shopping anchor. An Osaka hobby purchase can replace another planned purchase rather than becoming a new allowance.

**Clothing fit:** your saved reference is L tops and EU 44–46 trousers. Try garments or compare actual chest/shoulder/waist/inseam measurements; Japanese letter sizes and vintage labels vary. Her size is still unknown, so a bag or accessory is easier to choose than fitted clothing. Y2K/Korean-inspired items above are browsing ideas, not confirmed current branch products or a forecast of 2027 trends. Reuse [[Japan 2027 — Clothing to Browse Online]] and [[Japan 2027 — Gifts for Her]] for the detailed wishlist.

For perfume, retain the small-format options in [[Japan 2027 — Gifts for Her]] and test scents before buying. I have not verified a specific Osaka perfume counter or its stock in this chapter. A clothing-and-perfume basket must share her existing US$200 gift cap.

## Food and café plan

Try **takoyaki** as a small snack, **okonomiyaki** as a seated meal, and **kushikatsu** as an alternative dinner. These are established Osaka foods; my suggestion is to choose one main meal and one snack per outing rather than chase every dish. [Official Osaka food guide](https://osaka-info.jp/en/gourmet/).

**Kuromon Market** is an optional daytime food browse near Nipponbashi. Check displayed prices and choose a small serving before buying several items; it is not automatically the cheapest meal stop. [Official market overview](https://osaka-info.jp/en/osaka/food/gastronomy-markets/). **Dotonbori** works as the evening food-and-signage anchor. Choose a restaurant by the menu and wait that day; no restaurant reservation or price has been confirmed. [Dotonbori guide](https://www.osaka-info.jp/en/spot/dotonbori/).

LiLo is the named coffee lead for Minami. In Nakazakicho, leave a café slot flexible rather than assign an unverified opening time. Take-home sweets or coffee beans count under shopping; food consumed during the outing counts under meals.

## Minoh waterfall walk

Use **Hankyu Minoh Station** as the walking anchor, follow the signed waterfall route, and return to the station. Osaka's official tourism page describes roughly **40 minutes one way** and just under two hours for the round trip; my personal planning allowance is **2–3 hours for walking, rests, and photos**, plus transport and food. [Official Minoh Park guide](https://discover.osaka-info.jp/en/spots/mino-park) · [Tourism hiking map](https://minohkankou.net/wp-content/themes/minohkankou/pdf/2025/hikingmap-guide2025.pdf).

This is a suggested outing, not a verified current trail-condition report. Check access notices, weather, footwear needs, and daylight before choosing it. Stay with the main waterfall route unless you deliberately choose and prepare for a different trail. Avoid combining a full Minoh walk with a full Minami shopping day.

| Season | How I would adapt Osaka |
| --- | --- |
| Summer | Short outdoor blocks; lunch/café/indoor browsing during the hottest part of the day. Minoh still requires checking heat and weather; forest shade does not guarantee comfortable conditions. |
| November | Compare Minoh for autumn-color photography when local reports support it; finish the walk in daylight. Keep Dotonbori for city night photos. Peak foliage and special events are unconfirmed. |
| May | Choose greenery and waterfall/park photography, then evening food. The second half of May remains the comparison window in your May note; check actual holidays and prices before fixing dates. |

## Photography and filming wishlist

My short Osaka sequence: **city arrival → model/shop detail with permission → clothing texture → food preparation with permission → Dotonbori wide view → reflection or closing street scene**. The northern alternative uses café detail, a record sleeve, retro architecture, and a walking transition. The nature version uses a wide forest scene, flowing water, leaf detail, and ambient sound.

- Try Dotonbori as daylight fades, then compare the same composition after dark; crowds and weather determine the usable spot.
- In Amerikamura, look for signs, textures, and your own outfit/purchase rather than photograph strangers closely without permission.
- Ask before recording inside shops, cafés, or restaurants; follow posted restrictions and keep passageways clear.
- Keep one 30–90 second film goal, using [[Japan 2027 — Photography and Filming Wishlist|your existing filming outline]].

## Fit Osaka into the 15-day / 14-night guides

These are alternatives; **the current summer/autumn daily tables have not been replaced**. Choose a variant before creating dated cards or reservations.

| Variant | Night count | What changes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Kyoto day trip | Tokyo 4 + Kawaguchiko 2 + Kyoto 6 + Tokyo 2 = **14** | Replace one Kyoto outing, such as Day 11 or 12, with Minami. Return to the Kyoto hotel that night. |
| Two Osaka nights — my starting recommendation | Tokyo 4 + Kawaguchiko 2 + Kyoto 4 + Osaka 2 + Tokyo 2 = **14** | Days 1–6 unchanged; Day 7 Kyoto arrival; Days 8–10 choose three Kyoto outing blocks; Day 11 transfer to Osaka/Minami; Day 12 Kita OR Minoh; Day 13 Tokyo return; Day 14 shopping/packing; Day 15 departure. Two Kyoto nights are replaced. |
| Depart from Osaka/KIX | Tokyo 4 + Kawaguchiko 2 + Kyoto 5 + Osaka 3 = **14** | Days 1–6 unchanged; Days 7–11 Kyoto; Days 12–14 Osaka; Day 15 KIX departure. Move Tamiya to the first Tokyo block, replacing another outing. Compare the complete flight/rail/hotel cost before selecting. |

For the two-night variant, select Kyoto days deliberately: summer could retain Higashiyama, Arashiyama, and Uji; November could retain your three preferred foliage areas and fit tea into one lighter afternoon. You cannot preserve all six Kyoto day blocks in four nights. The separate Fuji summit branch has a hut and recovery days: rebuild its night balance if adding Osaka rather than applying this scenic-route table unchanged.

The original blossom guide already offers Osaka as an optional Kyoto excursion; this chapter expands that option without changing its March/April calendar.

## Transport and where to stay

**Namba/Shinsaibashi:** my first hotel-area comparison for Minami shopping and evening photos. **Osaka/Umeda:** compare if Kita, Kyoto access, and a Minoh outing are the priorities. Choose the actual hotel by total price, station exit/walking route, luggage practicality, and cancellation terms; no property is shortlisted or booked here.

**Shin-Osaka, Osaka/Umeda, and Namba are different station areas.** Match each intercity/local leg to the correct station before choosing a hotel. For a Kyoto day trip, compare routes from your actual Kyoto hotel using [JR West's official planning information](https://www.westjr.co.jp/global/en/); exact fares, travel times, and last-return services are not assigned in this chapter.

For a KIX departure, compare [JR airport access](https://www.westjr.co.jp/global/en/travel/shopping/access/train.html) and the [airport's Nankai access information](https://www.kansai-airport.or.jp/en/faq/2721), then verify the dated service, terminal, and airport arrival buffer. An Osaka departure is a flight comparison, not a confirmed change.

**Universal Studios Japan / Super Nintendo World:** optional separate full-day choice, replacing another Osaka outing. Park admission and area entry requirements need checking; purchasing a park ticket alone does not establish your chosen area-access arrangement. [Official timed-entry information](https://www.usj.co.jp/web/en/us/enjoy/numbered-ticket/timed-entry-ticket). Tickets, Express Pass options, dates, and cost are unconfirmed.

# 🐙 Osaka — pocket routes

[[Japan 2027 — Osaka Chapter|← Full Osaka chapter]] · [[Japan 2027 — Seasonal Planning and Bookings|Budget / bookings]]

**Undated alternatives.** Choose one per day; assign calendar dates only after the season and accommodation route are confirmed. Sequence and duration are my suggestions; opening times, train services, prices, and stock must be checked for the travel day. Official links, addresses, and map anchors are in the full chapter.

## Card 1 — Minami: models, clothes, food, neon

**Anchor:** Namba / Nipponbashi. **Best use:** one Kyoto day trip or your Osaka arrival day if you arrive with enough time.

| Block | Plan |
| --- | --- |
| Arrival / lunch | If arriving before shops open, start with a relaxed meal or orientation. Kuromon is an optional food browse. |
| First shopping block | Den Den Town; make Joshin Super Kids Land your main hobby stop. Choose one compact item to inspect. |
| Café / rest | Break before crossing to Shinsaibashi/Amerikamura; LiLo is the named coffee lead. |
| Clothing block | Pick two shops at most around Amerikamura/Shinsaibashi; WEGO is the verified branch lead. |
| Evening | Dotonbori signs and reflections, one dinner, then return to the hotel or Kyoto on a verified service. |

**Choose:** hobby + one fashion stop if time is short. **Buy idea:** one model OR one clothing piece; her accessory is an alternative gift purchase. **Shot:** wide neon scene → food detail with permission → souvenir detail. **Summer adjustment:** shorten outdoor walking and add a seated midday break. **Rain alternative:** concentrate on selected shops and a meal, with a shorter outdoor photo walk.

- [ ] Exact branch hours and item stock checked.
- [ ] Kyoto return connection / hotel route saved before dinner.
- [ ] Meals and shopping recorded once under their existing budget categories.

## Card 2 — Kita: cafés, records, fashion

**Anchor:** Osaka/Umeda. **Best use:** second Osaka day; city alternative to Minoh.

| Block | Plan |
| --- | --- |
| Start | Nakazakicho neighborhood walk and a café when open; leave the venue flexible. |
| Midday | Seated lunch and rest. |
| One main browse | DiskUnion Osaka in Doyamacho; choose a record/CD by interest and condition. |
| Optional second stop | HEP FIVE / SPINNS for her clothing or an accessory; omit if the main browse uses your shopping allowance. |
| Finish | Easy dinner near the chosen station or hotel; avoid adding another full Minami circuit. |

**Buy idea:** one record OR a gift accessory. **Shot:** retro street detail → café moment with permission → record sleeve you are considering. **Rain alternative:** reduce the neighborhood walk and focus on the selected indoor stops. **Exit:** check the actual station and platform for your hotel or onward train; Umeda complexes need orientation time.

- [ ] Café/store hours checked for the weekday.
- [ ] Her garment measurements known before buying fitted clothing.
- [ ] Record condition, luggage space, and final cost checked.

## Card 3 — Minoh: waterfall and greenery / foliage

**Anchor:** Hankyu Minoh Station. **Best use:** daylight nature day replacing Card 2, or the third day in the KIX variant.

| Block | Plan |
| --- | --- |
| Before leaving | Check weather, daylight, trail notices, station access, and return services. |
| Walk | Follow the main signed waterfall route; allow 2–3 hours for your walk/photos/rests, plus transport and food. |
| Waterfall | Wide scene, water/leaf detail, ambient sound; keep the route clear. |
| Return | Walk back to the station, eat, and keep the rest of the day light. |

**November:** choose only if actual foliage reports and trail conditions support the visit. **May:** focus on greenery. **Summer:** adapt to heat and rain; a forest outing still needs weather checks. **Bad-weather alternative:** Card 2 with shorter outdoor walking. **Buy idea:** none required; prioritize the outing over another shopping basket.

- [ ] Appropriate shoes, water, layers/rain protection chosen for conditions.
- [ ] Main-route access and daylight checked; no extra mountain trail assumed.
- [ ] Return to the station planned with buffer before onward travel.

**Optional USJ day:** replace one of these cards with a separate full park day only after checking admission, area-entry arrangements, and the total cost. No park ticket or Nintendo-area access is booked.
